# OpenAerialMap imagery, CC BY-NC 4.0

The 2,039 OpenAerialMap scenes licensed [CC BY-NC 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc/4.0/), mirrored from the [OpenAerialMap STAC API](https://api.imagery.hotosm.org/stac/collections/openaerialmap) on 2026-10-06. Scenes come from drones (604), aircraft (39) and satellites (1,396), uploaded since 2015 by individuals, mapping communities, governments and companies. Each scene is one STAC item whose Cloud Optimized GeoTIFF stays on OpenAerialMap's storage; items are grouped into one subcatalog per acquisition year. Search every scene at once through items.parquet. Each item names its own producer, who is the party to credit. Agents start at [AGENTS.md](https://source.coop/geospatialjeff/oam-portolan/oam-cc-by-nc-4-0/AGENTS.md).

## Quick Start

```python
import geopandas as gpd

gdf = gpd.read_parquet("./items.parquet")
print(gdf.head())
```

## STAC Metadata

- **root**: `../catalog.json`
- **parent**: `../catalog.json`

## Source

[https://imagery.hotosm.org/](https://imagery.hotosm.org/)

## Processing Notes

Harvested on 2026-10-06T17:48:03Z from the OpenAerialMap STAC API search endpoint (https://api.imagery.hotosm.org/stac/search, collection openaerialmap), paged in parallel by acquisition year and deduplicated by item id; the harvest matched the 21,863 items reported by OpenAerialMap's stats.json. Items were assigned to this collection by their license property; items with no license inherit CC-BY-4.0 from the upstream collection. Changes to the upstream item metadata: (1) email addresses removed from oam:uploader_email, provider descriptions and provider names, replaced with "Withheld (email address removed by mirror)" where the email was the whole name; (2) "datetime": null added to items that give only start_datetime and end_datetime, as STAC 1.1 requires the key; (3) one bbox with south and north swapped recomputed from its geometry; (4) http asset links rewritten to https on the same S3 object; (5) the unmodified upload asset ("original") given the source role instead of data; (6) structural links rewritten as relative links, with a canonical link to the upstream item. Imagery bytes are not copied or altered. The thumbnail plots scene centres over Natural Earth 1:110m land. items.parquet is generated with portolan stac-geoparquet, then Hilbert-sorted with gpio sort hilbert in 2,048-row groups.

## Known Issues

- This is a snapshot taken 2026-10-06T17:48:03Z. OpenAerialMap adds roughly 100 scenes a month; check the upstream STAC API for newer scenes.
- The gsd property is not reliable for every scene. Across the catalog, 114 of the 115 items with gsd below 0.0001 are EPSG:4326 COGs, so their gsd is in degrees. Every item in this collection has a gsd between 0.0001 and 10, but sibling collections do not. Filter on gsd with care.
- No item in this collection starts before 1990. 0 scenes have bounding boxes wider than 20 degrees, which inflates the collection extent.
- Older COGs have no mask. In a random sample of 40 scenes, 16 had no internal mask and 5 had no embedded band statistics. Render older scenes with nodata=0 to hide black borders.
- file:checksum is published upstream only for recent uploads, and file:size only for the COG. This mirror does not compute missing values for bytes it does not host.
- OpenAerialMap's bucket answers simple GET requests with Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *, but on 2026-10-06 it rejected a CORS preflight that asked to send a Range header (HTTP 403). Browser clients whose range reads need a preflight cannot read the COGs directly; server-side readers such as GDAL are unaffected.
